# Levo acceptance matrix (master spec §29)

The requirement → schema/service/API → user/admin UI → automated test → manual evidence → status matrix that spec §29 asks for. It covers the Levo Community + Studio master spec of 2026-10-19 (the Arabic master spec, 905 lines, supplied by the owner and kept outside the repository). Every section and tool of the spec has a row, including the items deferred for an owner decision or for hardware; nothing is folded into a summary sentence.

- **Not the old matrix:** `docs/ACCEPTANCE.md` is the acceptance matrix of an earlier mandate and stays untouched (gap map conflict 67).
- **Code base:** Phase 0 audited `b8233ee3`, the live default branch `claude/new-session-2hq4ci` on 2026-10-18. The refresh of 2026-10-20 re-checked, against `aa713842` (live), every row that the work landed since then could have moved; those rows carry `aa713842` evidence and are listed under [Refresh 2026-10-20](#refresh). Pushes 1.3 and 1.4 of the merchant approval gate (with merchant Wave 1 and migration `0205`) have landed and are live.
- **Sources:** 551 audited requirement rows (§03 to §30, with a verified sample corrected), 60 rows written for §00, §01, §02, §31, §32 and §33, the Community V3 and Studio 2.0 audits and the Shopify gap study (kept outside the repository), and the owner decisions: C1-C16 with C8 changed (DECISIONS rows 256-261), S1-S13, Q1-Q8 and the AI layer (DECISIONS row 262).
- **Companion documents:** `docs/LEVO_GAP_MAP.md` (architecture map, conflicts, blocked items, reference studies per track), `docs/LEVO_PLAN_PHASE1_2.md` (pushes P1-01 to P2-10 with their status) and `docs/LEVO_REFERENCE_MATRIX.md` (the reference study, with REA evidence ids).
- **How it moves:** from the 2026-10-20 refresh on, a code requirement moves to `done` only with a passing test cited. Document deliverables (§02, `29.00`, §32) are `done` when committed. Rows audited `done` at Phase 0 by code reading keep `—` until a test is attached. A real-device run or live probe still owed by a `done` row is listed under Manual evidence and must be recorded before the phase gate. The matrix is re-run at each phase gate (spec §29).

<a id="baseline"></a>
## Phase 0 baseline and live runs

### The run that deployed `aa713842`

Workflow 7 «7 - Deploy LIVE main site levonis-staging» ran on the push of [aa713842](https://github.com/justrandoomis/Levonis/commit/aa7138420115a810391a4cf2dd63000a8461fd80) to the default branch as [run 390](https://github.com/justrandoomis/Levonis/actions/runs/39344539808) (run id 39344539808), from 2026-10-19 23:55 to 2026-10-20 00:08 UTC. Its conclusion is **success**, and every job passed:

- **`verify`**, the reusable gate `.github/workflows/verify-all.yml` («CI - complete checks with sixteen root test shards»): `checks`, the root test shards 1/16 to 16/16, `workspace_tests` and `studio_tests`;
- **`fresh`**, which refuses a superseded release;
- **`deploy`**, which proves migrations on a throwaway database, sets a Time Travel restore point, applies pending migrations, deploys with the existing vars and runs the live probes.

Runs 388 and 389 on the same commits belong to a CI branch and were skipped by design; only a push to the default branch deploys.

### Test baseline

- **Gate:** the verify-all gate inside workflow 7 run 390 is the test baseline at `aa713842`: checks, 16 root shards, workspace tests and Studio tests, all green. Locally, `npm run check` and `npm run test:unit` run the same three suites before every push (CLAUDE.md).
- **Size:** at `aa713842`, `tests/` holds 1,151 `*.test.ts`/`*.test.mjs` files, `studio/tests/` holds 29 node suites plus the browser and perf scripts below, and `scripts/` holds 75 Playwright `e2e-*.mjs` scripts.

## How to read the matrix

| Column | Meaning |
|---|---|
| Requirement | The spec requirement in English. The spec wording is Arabic; Arabic terms are kept where the spec uses them. |
| Schema / Service / API | Code cited in the evidence under `migrations/`, `worker/`, `packages/`, `studio/worker/`, `studio/db/`, wrangler and workflow files. Extracted mechanically from the evidence; `—` means none was cited, which usually means nothing is built. |
| User / Admin UI | Code cited under `src/`, `studio/app/` and `public/`. |
| Automated test | Test files cited under `tests/`, `studio/tests/` and `scripts/e2e-*`. `—` means no test was found for this requirement. |
| Manual evidence | Earlier real runs (`docs/STUDIO_VERIFY.md`, `docs/PERFORMANCE_LOG.md`, `docs/STUDIO_IPAD_CHECKLIST.md`, `*.latest.json`, `verify-live-*`). The refresh cites the local Chromium runs recorded in `studio/tests/browser-*.latest.json`. «needed: …» names a real-device run or live probe that the row still owes (acceptance 01, 02, 03 and 06). No real-device run and no live direct-API probe has been recorded for this programme yet. |
| Run links | For a row proven by work landed since Phase 0: the commits (`a…b` is a range on the default branch) and the workflow 7 deploy run, plus the Studio deploy and verify runs for Studio work. For any other `done` row: the audit base `b8233ee3` and its deploy run 377. `—` for a row that is not done and that no landed commit has touched since Phase 0. «P1-14» marks the rows this prose push completes. |
| Status | `done`: the requirement holds in live code. `partial`: some of it holds. `missing`: nothing holds. `conflict`: the spec contradicts a recorded owner decision or rule, and the owner must choose. `blocked-external`: needs hardware, a paid service or an owner money/legal decision outside the code. |
| Evidence | path:line citations at `b8233ee3`, or at `aa713842` for the rows re-checked on 2026-10-20. Line numbers in older rows may have shifted where a file changed since Phase 0. |
| Next | The next concrete step, with spec phase (P0-P7) and size (S/M/L/XL). Plan pushes (P1-01 to P2-10) are mapped per acceptance test below and per requirement in `docs/LEVO_PLAN_PHASE1_2.md`. |

## Status after the owner's AI-layer decision

The owner decided the **AI layer** on 2026-10-19 while this audit ran (now DECISIONS row 262):
- Cloudflare Workers AI (binding `AI`, no key);
- the Claude API from the Worker (secret `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`), behind a provider-agnostic adapter with per-capability routing, a hard daily spend ceiling and a basic mode;
- binding limits: a model never states a price, stock, compatibility verdict or HMS meaning, and never writes Sorani on its own.

The only earlier owner text on paid keys is the same day's Smart Diagnose answer (built without any paid AI key), which still holds. Image-to-3D still needs a GPU host. Three rows change status, and the counts below use the current status:

| Row | Audited status | Current status | Why |
|---|---|---|---|
| `24.provider-boundary` | blocked-external | missing | Audited as blocked-external (no provider approved). The AI-layer decision 2026-10-19 (DECISIONS row 262): Cloudflare Workers AI (binding AI) and the Claude API (secret ANTHROPIC_API_KEY) behind a provider-agnostic adapter with a daily spend ceiling; a model never states a price, stock, compatibility verdict or HMS meaning and never writes Sorani on its own. The blocker is gone; the adapter, budgets, retention statement and KYC/private-file exclusion tests are now buildable work. |
| `29.44.ai-no-kyc-no-injection` | blocked-external | missing | Audited as blocked-external (no AI). The AI-layer decision 2026-10-19 (DECISIONS row 262): Cloudflare Workers AI (binding AI) and the Claude API (secret ANTHROPIC_API_KEY) behind a provider-agnostic adapter with a daily spend ceiling; a model never states a price, stock, compatibility verdict or HMS meaning and never writes Sorani on its own. These tests become mandatory when the AI layer (smart search and assistant lane) ships. |
| `29.45.ai-drafts-needs-approval` | blocked-external | missing | Audited as blocked-external (no AI). The AI-layer decision 2026-10-19 (DECISIONS row 262): Cloudflare Workers AI (binding AI) and the Claude API (secret ANTHROPIC_API_KEY) behind a provider-agnostic adapter with a daily spend ceiling; a model never states a price, stock, compatibility verdict or HMS meaning and never writes Sorani on its own. Human-approval tokens on sensitive mutations must ship with the AI layer. |

Seven more rows carry an "AI layer" note in their Next column with no status change: `04.visual-search`, `11.1-input`, `13.no-paid-api`, `24.assistant-base`, `24.injection-safe`, `23.kb-code-or-photo`, `09.compute-ai-queue`.

## The six demo journeys (§30, spec lines 776-797)

A journey is `done` only when every step is done, and `missing` when no step holds.

| Journey | Steps done / partial / missing | Journey status | Main blockers | Earliest phase |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| A: Store medals (logo to medal, batch, per-merchant price) | 2 / 2 / 1 | partial | no generator (A1); no Production Package (A4); per-merchant auto quotes (A2) | P2 |
| B: Clicker from a private template (designer rights, catalogue part) | 0 / 1 / 3 | partial | no template model, locked zones or customiser; royalty policy is an owner decision (B3) | P2 (rights: owner) |
| C: Drawer organiser with realistic preview (plate, AR true scale) | 0 / 3 / 1 | partial | no organiser generator; no separate manufacture-scale control | P2 (AR: P5) |
| D: Free multi-source file (parts, needs review, comparable offers) | 1 / 2 / 1 | partial | request model is single-source; no needs-review state; no per-part coverage | P1 |
| E: High-privacy design (server render, manufacture only) | 1 / 1 / 2 | partial | no privacy tiers; no server render host (blocked-external) | P5 |
| F: Executable community project (steps, BOM, Make, qualified review) | 1 / 4 / 0 | partial | no steps, BOM, licence or Make type; community closed by owner | P4 |

Journeys D and F assume a public community. Owner answer Q1 (DECISIONS row 262): no allow-list; the journeys are built open to everyone, and the community and `/requests` open only when the owner opens them, after every task is complete. Until then the journeys are tested behind the closed gate (`tests/communityGate.test.ts:159`) and nothing is launched publicly.

done 12 · partial 9 · blocked-external 1

Do not add a second SSO. (P1, S) |
